**Slips:** p.2 heading uses awkward preposition: "• Problems by Using Basic Gates" instead of "Problems of Using" or "Problems with Using"; p.2 bullet has subject-verb agreement slips: "As no. of components rise, nodes interconnection complexity grow exponentially" ("rise" with singular "no.", and "grow" with singular "complexity"). p.3 bullet has awkward phrasing: "– require to keep a significant inventory of ICs" instead of "require keeping". p.4 table entry has subject-verb agreement slip: "Hardware can configure any time user need to only by programming." (missing "s" in "user need to"). p.8 bullet has grammatical slip: "Can typically replaced several fixed function SSI or MSI devices..." (past participle "replaced" instead of base verb "replace"); p.8 bullet has subject-verb agreement slip: "A typical CPLD is the equivalent of from 2 to 64 SPLDs and come in 44 pins to 160 pin packages..." ("come" instead of "comes"). p.9 bullet 2 omits subject: "• Have the greatest logic capacity." (missing "They have"); p.9 bullet 3 has awkward phrasing: "thousands of logic gates groups" instead of "groups of logic gates"; p.9 bullet 4 has grammatical slip: "Two basics classes of FPGA..." ("basics" instead of "basic"). p.10 bullet 3 misnames processor family: "Xilinx Vertex-E" instead of Xilinx Virtex-E. p.21 read example states: "For input (A2,A1,A0) = 011, output is (F3,F2,F1,F0 ) = 0011." But input 011 selects D3, which has no connections in the grid, yielding output 0000; the stated output 0011 corresponds to input 001 (D1 where F1=1 and F0=1). p.24 bullet 1 states: "4-input, 3-output PAL with fixed, 3-input OR terms", but the circuit and problem statement clearly have 4 outputs (F1, F2, F3, F4). p.25 writes lowercase function `y` as uppercase `Y` in `Y = A’B + CD + B’D’`. p.28 specifies functions F2 and F3 as 3-variable functions `(A, B, C)`, but lists minterms up to 11 and 15 (`10-11` and `9-15`), which require 4 variables `(A, B, C, D)`. p.29 bullet 3 has awkward phrasing: "The output doesn’t change and connect to 0 (since x⊕0 = x)" instead of "when connected to 0".

### 1.16 `PYQ-MTE-2025-09-25.pdf` (2 pp.)

**Paper Metadata:**
- **Institution:** MANIPAL UNIVERSITY JAIPUR, Faculty of Science, Technology & Architecture, School of Engineering, Department of Electronics & Communication Engineering
- **Program / Course:** B.Tech. (Electronics & Communication Engineering), Digital Electronics [ECE2102]
- **Exam:** Odd Semester Mid Term Examination, September 2025
- **Exam Date & Time:** 25-Sep-2025 (11:30 AM - 01:00 PM)
- **Duration:** 90 mins.
- **Marks:** 30
- **Instructions:** All questions are compulsory. Missing data, if any, may be assumed suitably.

---

#### SECTION A (Memory Based Questions)
Answer all the questions.

- **MTE Q1** (2 marks):
  > Optimized the following expression and implement using 2-input NOR gate.  
  > F = P'Q' +P'

- **MTE Q2** (2 marks):
  > The four-bit binary sequence 1011 is applied to an even parity generator circuit. What will be the output?

- **MTE Q3** (2 marks):
  > What excitation input should be applied to a T- FF to make output toggle?

---

#### SECTION B (Concept Based Questions)
Answer all the questions.

- **MTE Q4** (4 marks):
  > Minimize the following Boolean function using K-Map method to obtain a simplified expression. Also draw the logic circuit using basic gates.  
  > F(a,b,c,d) = Σm (0, 4,5, 7- 9,15) + Σd (1,3,6,14)  
  *(Note: "7- 9" is printed as a range representing minterms 7, 8, 9).*

- **MTE Q5** (4 marks):
  > Implement the following function using single 8:1 Multiplexer.  
  > F(P,Q,R,S) = Σm (0, 1,3, 4, 8,9,10,11,13,15)

- **MTE Q6** (4 marks):
  > Design Full Adder using suitable decoder.

- **MTE Q7** (4 marks):
  > Draw the output waveform for the following input waveform, assuming the flip-flop to be positive edge triggered and the output Q is initially at logic zero.

  **Figure description (Timing Diagram):**
  - **Inputs:** Three waveform traces labeled on the left: `CLK` (top), `J` (middle), and `K` (bottom), corresponding to a positive edge-triggered JK flip-flop.
  - **Clock (`CLK`):** 7 consecutive square-wave clock cycles / pulses (numbered 1 through 7), 50% duty cycle with period $T$ (HIGH for $T/2$, LOW for $T/2$).
  - **Grid / Vertical Dashed Lines:** Vertical dotted lines mark all clock edges as well as intermediate input transition edges:
    - Cycle 1: Rising edge 1, falling edge 1, and midpoint of LOW phase (where K transitions).
    - Cycle 2: Rising edge 2 (where K transitions), midpoint of HIGH phase (where K transitions), falling edge 2, and midpoint of LOW phase (where K transitions).
    - Cycle 3: Rising edge 3, falling edge 3.
    - Cycle 4: Rising edge 4, midpoint of HIGH phase (where J transitions), falling edge 4.
    - Cycle 5: Rising edge 5, midpoint of HIGH phase (where K transitions), falling edge 5 (where J and K transition), and midpoint of LOW phase (where K transitions).
    - Cycle 6: Rising edge 6, falling edge 6 (where J transitions).
    - Cycle 7: Rising edge 7, falling edge 7 (where J and K transition).
  - **Trace Transitions and Levels by Clock Cycle:**
    - **Cycle 1:**
      - At Rising Edge 1 ($t = 0$): `CLK` 0→1, `J` 0→1, `K` = 0.
      - During CLK HIGH ($0 \le t < T/2$): `J` = 1, `K` = 0.
      - At CLK Falling Edge ($t = T/2$): `CLK` 1→0; `J` falls 1→0; `K` = 0.
      - During first half of CLK LOW ($T/2 \le t < 3T/4$): `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
      - At $t = 3T/4$ (midway through LOW phase): `K` rises 0→1.
      - During second half of CLK LOW ($3T/4 \le t < T$): `J` = 0, `K` = 1.
    - **Cycle 2:**
      - At Rising Edge 2 ($t = T$): `CLK` 0→1; `K` falls 1→0 at the edge; `J` = 0.
      - During first half of CLK HIGH ($T \le t < T + T/4$): `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
      - At $t = T + T/4$ (midway through HIGH phase): `K` rises 0→1.
      - During second half of CLK HIGH ($T + T/4 \le t < T + T/2$): `J` = 0, `K` = 1.
      - At CLK Falling Edge ($t = T + T/2$): `CLK` 1→0; `J` = 0, `K` remains 1.
      - During first half of CLK LOW ($T + T/2 \le t < T + 3T/4$): `J` = 0, `K` = 1.
      - At $t = T + 3T/4$ (midway through LOW phase): `K` falls 1→0.
      - During second half of CLK LOW ($T + 3T/4 \le t < 2T$): `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
    - **Cycle 3:**
      - At Rising Edge 3 ($t = 2T$): `CLK` 0→1; `J` rises 0→1; `K` = 0.
      - During CLK HIGH ($2T \le t < 2T + T/2$): `J` = 1, `K` = 0.
      - At CLK Falling Edge ($t = 2T + T/2$): `CLK` 1→0; `J` falls 1→0; `K` = 0.
      - During CLK LOW ($2T + T/2 \le t < 3T$): `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
    - **Cycle 4:**
      - At Rising Edge 4 ($t = 3T$): `CLK` 0→1; `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
      - During first half of CLK HIGH ($3T \le t < 3T + T/4$): `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
      - At $t = 3T + T/4$ (midway through HIGH phase): `J` rises 0→1.
      - During second half of CLK HIGH ($3T + T/4 \le t < 3T + T/2$): `J` = 1, `K` = 0.
      - At CLK Falling Edge ($t = 3T + T/2$): `CLK` 1→0; `J` remains 1; `K` = 0.
      - During CLK LOW ($3T + T/2 \le t < 4T$): `J` = 1, `K` = 0.
    - **Cycle 5:**
      - At Rising Edge 5 ($t = 4T$): `CLK` 0→1; `J` = 1, `K` = 0.
      - During first half of CLK HIGH ($4T \le t < 4T + T/4$): `J` = 1, `K` = 0.
      - At $t = 4T + T/4$ (midway through HIGH phase): `K` rises 0→1.
      - During second half of CLK HIGH ($4T + T/4 \le t < 4T + T/2$): `J` = 1, `K` = 1.
      - At CLK Falling Edge ($t = 4T + T/2$): `CLK` 1→0; `J` falls 1→0; `K` falls 1→0.
      - During first half of CLK LOW ($4T + T/2 \le t < 4T + 3T/4$): `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
      - At $t = 4T + 3T/4$ (midway through LOW phase): `K` rises 0→1.
      - During second half of CLK LOW ($4T + 3T/4 \le t < 5T$): `J` = 0, `K` = 1.
    - **Cycle 6:**
      - At Rising Edge 6 ($t = 5T$): `CLK` 0→1; `J` = 0, `K` = 1.
      - During CLK HIGH ($5T \le t < 5T + T/2$): `J` = 0, `K` = 1.
      - At CLK Falling Edge ($t = 5T + T/2$): `CLK` 1→0; `J` rises 0→1; `K` remains 1.
      - During CLK LOW ($5T + T/2 \le t < 6T$): `J` = 1, `K` = 1.
    - **Cycle 7:**
      - At Rising Edge 7 ($t = 6T$): `CLK` 0→1; `J` = 1, `K` = 1.
      - During CLK HIGH ($6T \le t < 6T + T/2$): `J` = 1, `K` = 1.
      - At CLK Falling Edge ($t = 6T + T/2$): `CLK` 1→0; `J` falls 1→0; `K` falls 1→0.
      - Remainder of LOW phase ($t > 6T + T/2$): `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
  - **Summary of Values at Positive (Sampling) Edges & Resulting Q:**
    - Initial state: $Q = 0$.
    - **Edge 1:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
    - **Edge 2:** $J = 0, K = 1$ (prior to edge $K=1$, falling at edge) $\implies Q$ transitions to **0** (Reset).
    - **Edge 3:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
    - **Edge 4:** $J = 0, K = 0 \implies Q$ holds at **1** (No change).
    - **Edge 5:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ remains at **1** (Set).
    - **Edge 6:** $J = 0, K = 1 \implies Q$ transitions to **0** (Reset).
    - **Edge 7:** $J = 1, K = 1 \implies Q$ toggles to **1** (Toggle).
  - **ASCII Timing Diagram:**
    ```text
    CLK:  |‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_
    Edge: ^1    ^2    ^3    ^4    ^5    ^6    ^7

    J:    |‾‾|_______|‾‾|____/‾‾‾‾‾‾|‾‾|_____/‾‾‾‾|‾‾|_

    K:    ____/‾|_/‾‾‾‾\_________|‾\_/‾|‾‾‾‾‾‾|‾‾|_

    Q:    ___/‾‾‾\___/‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾\_____/‾‾‾‾‾
    ```

- **PS2 Q3** (marks: not specified):
  > 3. Input waveforms for JK flip-flops are shown in given figures. Draw the output waveform for each case. Assume the flip-flops to be negative edge triggered for both cases and the output Q is initially at logic zero.  
  > a.  
  > b.

  **Figure description (Sub-figure a):**
  - **Inputs:** Three waveform rows labeled on the left: `CLK` (top), `J` (middle), and `K` (bottom). Negative-edge triggered JK flip-flop without asynchronous inputs. Output initially $Q = 0$.
  - **Clock (`CLK`):** 7 consecutive square-wave clock cycles / pulses (numbered 1 through 7) with 50% duty cycle, period $T \approx 35\text{ px}$. Negative (falling) edges occur at $t = T/2, 3T/2, 5T/2, 7T/2, 9T/2, 11T/2, 13T/2$. Vertical dotted guide lines are drawn downward from each falling edge and input transition.
  - **Trace Transitions and Levels by Clock Cycle:**
    - **Cycle 1:**
      - At pulse 1 rising edge: `J` rises 0→1; `K` = 0.
      - During pulse 1 HIGH phase: `J` = 1, `K` = 0.
      - At **Falling Edge 1**: `CLK` 1→0; `J` falls 1→0 right at the edge; `K` = 0. Sampling inputs $J=1, K=0 \implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
      - During pulse 1 LOW phase: `K` has a positive pulse from midway to near the end of the LOW phase.
    - **Cycle 2:**
      - During pulse 2 HIGH phase: `J` = 0; `K` rises 0→1 before the falling edge.
      - At **Falling Edge 2**: `CLK` 1→0; `J` = 0, `K` = 1. Sampling inputs $J=0, K=1 \implies Q$ transitions to **0** (Reset).
      - During pulse 2 LOW phase: `K` falls 1→0; `J` = 0.
    - **Cycle 3:**
      - At pulse 3 rising edge: `J` rises 0→1; `K` = 0.
      - During pulse 3 HIGH phase: `J` = 1, `K` = 0.
      - At **Falling Edge 3**: `CLK` 1→0; `J` falls 1→0 right at the edge; `K` = 0. Sampling inputs $J=1, K=0 \implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
      - During pulse 3 LOW phase: `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
    - **Cycle 4:**
      - During pulse 4 HIGH phase: `J` rises 0→1 midway through; `K` = 0.
      - At **Falling Edge 4**: `CLK` 1→0; `J` = 1, `K` = 0. Sampling inputs $J=1, K=0 \implies Q$ remains at **1** (Set / Hold 1).
      - During pulse 4 LOW phase: `J` remains 1; `K` rises 0→1 near the end of the cycle.
    - **Cycle 5:**
      - During pulse 5 HIGH phase: both `J` = 1 and `K` = 1.
      - At **Falling Edge 5**: `CLK` 1→0; `J` and `K` both fall 1→0 right at the edge. Sampling inputs prior to edge $J=1, K=1 \implies Q$ toggles to **0** (Toggle 1→0).
      - During pulse 5 LOW phase: `K` has a brief positive pulse; `J` = 0.
    - **Cycle 6:**
      - During pulse 6 HIGH phase: `K` has a pulse; `J` rises 0→1 near the edge; `K` rises 0→1 near the edge.
      - At **Falling Edge 6**: `CLK` 1→0; both `J` = 1 and `K` = 1. Sampling inputs $J=1, K=1 \implies Q$ toggles to **1** (Toggle 0→1).
      - During pulse 6 LOW phase: `J` and `K` both fall 1→0.
    - **Cycle 7:**
      - During pulse 7 HIGH phase: `J` = 0, `K` = 0.
      - At **Falling Edge 7**: `CLK` 1→0; `J` = 0, `K` = 0. Sampling inputs $J=0, K=0 \implies Q$ holds at **1** (No change).
  - **Summary of Values at Negative Edges & Resulting Q (Figure a):**
    - Initial state: $Q = 0$.
    - **Edge 1:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
    - **Edge 2:** $J = 0, K = 1 \implies Q$ transitions to **0** (Reset).
    - **Edge 3:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
    - **Edge 4:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ remains at **1** (Set).
    - **Edge 5:** $J = 1, K = 1 \implies Q$ toggles to **0** (Toggle).
    - **Edge 6:** $J = 1, K = 1 \implies Q$ toggles to **1** (Toggle).
    - **Edge 7:** $J = 0, K = 0 \implies Q$ holds at **1** (No change).
  - **ASCII Timing Diagram (Figure a):**
    ```text
    CLK:   |‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_
    Edge:     ↓1    ↓2    ↓3    ↓4    ↓5    ↓6    ↓7

    J:     |‾‾|_______|‾‾|____/‾‾‾‾‾‾|‾‾|_____/‾‾‾‾|_____

    K:     ____/‾|__/‾‾‾‾\_______/‾‾‾|‾‾|__/‾|_/‾‾‾|_____

    Q:     ___/‾‾‾‾‾\_____/‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾\_____/‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾
    ```

  **Figure description (Sub-figure b):**
  - **Inputs:** Five waveform rows labeled on the left: `CLK` (top), `J` (second), `K` (third), `CLR` (fourth, active-LOW asynchronous Clear), and `PR` (fifth, active-LOW asynchronous Preset). Negative-edge triggered JK flip-flop. Output initially $Q = 0$.
  - **Clock (`CLK`):** 7 consecutive square-wave clock cycles / pulses with period $T \approx 44\text{ px}$. Negative (falling) edges occur at the trailing edges of pulses 1 through 6.
  - **Asynchronous Inputs Behavior:**
    - `CLR` is normally HIGH (inactive, logic 1). It has two active-LOW pulses ($\text{CLR} = 0$):
      1. Before Pulse 1 ($t < t_{\text{pulse 1}}$): A narrow active-LOW pulse on `CLR` asynchronously resets the flip-flop ($Q = 0$).
      2. Around Pulse 5 ($t \approx t_{\text{edge 5}}$): An active-LOW pulse spanning across Falling Edge 5 asynchronously forces $Q = 0$, overriding synchronous inputs.
    - `PR` is normally HIGH (inactive, logic 1). It has one active-LOW pulse ($\text{PR} = 0$):
      1. Between Edge 3 and Edge 4 (during the LOW phase after pulse 3): A narrow active-LOW pulse asynchronously presets the flip-flop ($Q = 1$).
  - **Trace Transitions and Levels by Clock Cycle:**
    - **Pre-Clock 1:** Active-LOW pulse on `CLR` forces $Q = 0$.
    - **Pulse 1:**
      - During pulse 1: `J` is HIGH (1); `K` has a pulse to HIGH and returns to LOW right at the falling edge; `CLR` = 1, `PR` = 1.
      - At **Falling Edge 1**: `J` = 1, `K` = 0 (or toggle if sampled during trailing pulse) $\implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
    - **Pulse 2:**
      - During pulse 2: `J` = 1, `K` = 0; `CLR` = 1, `PR` = 1.
      - At **Falling Edge 2**: `J` = 1, `K` = 0 $\implies Q$ remains at **1** (Set).
    - **Pulse 3:**
      - During pulse 3: `J` = 1, `K` = 0; `CLR` = 1, `PR` = 1.
      - At **Falling Edge 3**: `J` = 1, `K` = 0 $\implies Q$ remains at **1** (Set).
    - **Post-Pulse 3 Asynchronous Preset:**
      - During the LOW phase between pulse 3 and pulse 4: `PR` drops to 0 (active-LOW preset pulse). This asynchronously asserts $Q = 1$ ($Q$ was already 1, remains 1).
    - **Pulse 4:**
      - During pulse 4: `J` = 0, `K` = 0; `CLR` = 1, `PR` = 1.
      - At **Falling Edge 4**: `J` = 0, `K` = 0 $\implies Q$ holds at **1** (No change).
    - **Pulse 5 & Asynchronous Clear:**
      - During pulse 5 and across Falling Edge 5: `CLR` drops to 0 (active-LOW clear pulse). Asynchronous reset overrides clocking and forces $Q = 0$ immediately.
      - At **Falling Edge 5**: `CLR` is active-LOW (0) $\implies Q = 0$.
    - **Pulse 6:**
      - `CLR` returns to 1 (inactive). `PR` = 1 (inactive).
      - During pulse 6: `J` = 0, `K` = 1.
      - At **Falling Edge 6**: `J` = 0, `K` = 1 $\implies Q$ remains at **0** (Reset).
    - **Pulse 7:**
      - After Edge 6, `J` rises to 1; `K` = 0.
      - At Falling Edge 7 (if completed): `J` = 1, `K` = 0 $\implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
  - **Summary of Events & Resulting Q (Figure b):**
    - Initial: $Q = 0$.
    - **Pre-Clock:** $\text{CLR} = 0 \implies Q = 0$ (Asynchronous Clear).
    - **Edge 1:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
    - **Edge 2:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ remains at **1** (Set).
    - **Edge 3:** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ remains at **1** (Set).
    - **Mid-Cycle (post-Edge 3):** $\text{PR} = 0 \implies Q = 1$ (Asynchronous Preset).
    - **Edge 4:** $J = 0, K = 0 \implies Q$ holds at **1** (No change).
    - **Edge 5 window:** $\text{CLR} = 0 \implies Q$ forced to **0** (Asynchronous Clear).
    - **Edge 6:** $J = 0, K = 1 \implies Q$ remains at **0** (Reset).
    - **Edge 7 (if clocked):** $J = 1, K = 0 \implies Q$ transitions to **1** (Set).
  - **ASCII Timing Diagram (Figure b):**
    ```text
    CLK:  |‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_|‾‾|_
    Edge:    ↓1    ↓2    ↓3    ↓4    ↓5    ↓6    ↓7

    J:    |‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾|_____|‾‾‾‾‾|___________/‾‾‾‾

    K:    ___/‾|___/‾‾‾‾\___________/‾|___/‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾

    CLR:  _/\‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾\___/‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾

    PR:   ‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾\___/‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾

    Q:    ___/‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾‾\___________________
    ```

- **PS2 Q4** (marks: not specified):
  > 4. Design 2-bit synchronous up counter.

- **PS2 Q5** (marks: not specified):
  > 5. Design 2-bit synchronous down counter using TFF.

- **PS2 Q6** (marks: not specified):
  > 6. Design 3-bit synchronous down counter using DFF.
